<a id="e-effect-light-shafts"></a>
### E-EFFECT-LIGHT-SHAFTS: 历史 strict direct-draw 与当前 authored Program migration

- 实现提交：strict Linear/Gradient `49a5878`，几何/UV 修正 `38cd1d3`，连续多光束近似 `c47fe22`，strict Radial/Color `4622b8bb`。代码：[SceneAuthoredLightShaftsPlanner.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/SceneAuthoredLightShaftsPlanner.swift)、[SceneAuthoredLightShaftsPlanner+AssetValidation.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/SceneAuthoredLightShaftsPlanner+AssetValidation.swift)、[SceneLightShaftsExecutionPlan.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/SceneLightShaftsExecutionPlan.swift)、[SceneLightShaftsPipeline.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Effects/SceneLightShaftsPipeline.swift)、[SceneLightShaftsEffectTextureLoader.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Resources/SceneLightShaftsEffectTextureLoader.swift) 与共享 direct-draw 路径。实现不含样本 ID 分支；作者 `point0...3` 只构成 effect-local perspective UV，退化、奇异或单位 quad 内分母跨零的映射失败关闭。
- 准入：只接受 exact stock definition/material/vertex+fragment raw/canonical SHA-256、单 effect/单 material/无 authored RT 的 standalone quad、normal/no-depth/no-write/nocull 和完整静态常量集合。当前只开放两组互斥 tuple：Linear/Gradient 为默认 `RAYMODE=0 + DIRECTDRAW=1 + RENDERING=1`；Radial/Color 为 `RAYMODE=1 + DIRECTDRAW=1`，要求 `0 <= rayzstartangle <= rayzzendangle <= 1`，并允许该真实 profile 的 exponent 0。实例纹理/user binding、child/dependency、inline script、prior-effect input、Corner/混合 combo、unknown field/state/hash 均拒绝。noise 仍按 project-local 优先、项目 stock 回退；只有 Gradient profile 必须取得 gradient texture。
- 官方与 clean-room 边界：[Wallpaper Engine Light Shafts 文档](https://docs.wallpaperengine.io/en/scene/effects/effect/lightshafts.html) 只确认 Linear/Radial/Corner 与 Color/Gradient 的作者模式边界，没有公开 standalone quad 的精确局部 bounds 或私有 shader 公式。Linear 仍由项目独立的纵向 Gaussian 光带近似；Radial/Color 用作者 angle 范围、scale、radius、noise、smoothness、speed、intensity、起止颜色与 perspective UV 生成有界扇形光束。两者保持 premultiplied 半透明输出，不复制或声称恢复官方私有数学、束数或节奏。
- 自动门：[test_scene_light_shafts_geometry.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_light_shafts_geometry.py)、[test_scene_light_shafts_planner.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_light_shafts_planner.py)、[test_scene_light_shafts_rendering.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_light_shafts_rendering.py)、[test_scene_light_shafts_texture_loader.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_light_shafts_texture_loader.py)、[test_scene_framebuffer_capture.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_framebuffer_capture.py) 及 chain/repository/benchmark 门。Light Shafts 直接聚焦 **38/38 PASS**，benchmark/layout 合集 **61/61 PASS**，Scene **150 modules ALL OK**；Radial/Color GPU 门锁四时相持续、双侧覆盖、至少三束、质心运动、上宽下窄、作者紫色、无需 gradient、premultiplied 与半透明，Linear 继续要求 gradient，错误 combo/角域/资源失败关闭。
- 运行门：最终 full45 `.codex/scene-light-shafts-radial-full45-20260801-v1/report.json` **45/45 PASS**，Light Shafts 3、stage/chain `318/57`、graph failed layer/failed frame/drawable miss `0/0/0`。三个真实正例为 `3299228616`、`3766387484`、`3768903841`，各执行 1 个 Light Shafts；烟花样本 layer 162 进入 succeeded 集合，样本 stage/chain `12/1`，CPU/GPU p95 `1.885/8.288 ms`、driver `59.848 fps`。fixed13 同构建 **13/13 PASS**。报告、矩阵与 App 身份见本页统一门；性能数字只作本机观测。
- 视觉边界：官方三张截图与最终 ready/after 都可见烟花升空/爆炸、横向暗云和随时间变化的底部/径向光带，证明缺失的 Radial/Color layer 已进入可见共享执行路径。但当前烟花明显过曝、五实例同步、整体偏灰蓝，官方图中的暖白/粉紫层次、不同爆炸阶段和射灯强度/分布没有对齐；preview 指标仍是 advisory。该证据不证明 exact half-canvas extent、束数、角度、节奏、色彩空间、Color Grading/Workshop Tint 或 Windows 像素/动态一致；Corner、其他 combo、mask、non-direct-draw、ordered-chain 输入继续失败关闭。
- 现役owner说明：以上direct-draw提交、full45与视觉偏差是历史oracle，不再代表所有当前正例。真实`3766387484:80#effect#81`的material只显式给`DIRECTDRAW=1, RENDERING=1`，`RAYMODE`由active annotation default补为0，故它是exact **Linear/Gradient** standalone profile，不是此前误记的Radial/Color；Program实际绑定`util/noise`与`gradient/gradient_iridescent`。R4-3已让该profile在完整Program成功时由authored source / resolved-material graph唯一持有；B15删除完整authored graph的旧whole-chain产品路由，B16又删除无resolved frame plan时的独立quad main-pass bypass。Light Shafts当前只有resolved `transparentDirectDraw`产品owner；unsupported/no-claim形态不绘制，旧renderer仅保留为R5删除面和测试oracle。当前迁移证据、官方combo-default边界与最终源码v48报告见[E-R4-3-AUTHORED-PROGRAM-CHAIN](#e-r4-3-authored-program-chain)，撤权证据见[E-R4-B16-LEGACY-LIGHT-SHAFTS-MAIN-PASS-RETIREMENT](#e-r4-b16-legacy-light-shafts-main-pass-retirement)；v48仍呈宽幅暖橙/蓝白光带，且完整画面动效未闭合。B16未刷新benchmark/fixed13/full45；尚未证明三个历史正例全部迁移，因此不能把该单样本checkpoint或静态撤权写成Light Shafts family完成或视觉等价。

<a id="e-effect-opacity"></a>
### E-EFFECT-OPACITY: unified material Program 与 typed optional mask

- owner 状态：普通 Opacity Program 先由统一 capability 编译并进入 graph executor；编译成功的 resolved subject 从旧 catalog 排除，同一 stage 不会双 owner。Opacity 不在 `supportsUnifiedPairLeaf` 白名单内；有authored graph但无resolved claim时，B15在compositor入口失败关闭，不再由旧whole-chain renderer执行或释放给bounded planner。只有根本没有 authored graph 的层才继续由历史 `b8842d8` planner/pipeline/renderer 服务；旧chain专用测试只是像素oracle。B22已撤销独立ImageBlend owner；Clipping Mask→static Opacity的exact external-primary形态现由B12统一owner执行，legacy Procedural Noise已由B13统一owner执行。现役统一入口是 [SceneAuthoredShaderFrontend.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/ShaderFrontend/SceneAuthoredShaderFrontend.swift)、[SceneAuthoredShaderDeadBindingAnalyzer.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/ShaderFrontend/SceneAuthoredShaderDeadBindingAnalyzer.swift)、[SceneResolvedMaterialProgramFinalizer.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/MaterialProgram/SceneResolvedMaterialProgramFinalizer.swift)、[SceneResolvedMaterialExecutionCapability.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/EffectExecution/SceneResolvedMaterialExecutionCapability.swift) 与 [SceneResolvedMaterialGraphExecutor.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/EffectExecution/SceneResolvedMaterialGraphExecutor.swift)。
- clean-room 语义：官方公开 Opacity 语义仍是 `g_UserAlpha` 与 optional opacity mask；本地官方客户端静态资料只用于确认 MASK 分支、per-slot resolution 与 effect-instance 归属，不复制 shader/payload。项目自己的 premultiplied pipeline保持 RGB/alpha 同比例变化；52 包语料中同层多个 Opacity 实例可绑定不同 mask，因此资源 identity 必须保持到 effect/slot，而不能退化为 per-layer 单值。
- 公共编译合同：prepared variant 未实际引用的 sampler 从 active GPU schema 删除；对应 resolution uniform 只有在全部引用仅写入 fragment 不消费的 varying 分量时才可证明为 dead。position、fragment live varying、未知调用或其他活数据流继续 fail closed。optional mask 的启动 fixed-point 复用 launch envelope 中所有 reachable variant 的 typed sampler schema；只有同一 reference 在所有 reachable schema 中推导出唯一一致 purpose 时，runtime 才可在 `MASK=0` bootstrap 阶段判定 mask readiness 并重编译 `MASK=1`。purpose 缺失/歧义、资源未 ready 或非法 variant 均不伪造白纹理或 metadata。
- mixed-chain 边界：resolved prefix 后只能接 `supportsUnifiedPairLeaf` 明确允许的 dedicated leaf；空 allowlist 表示没有可接管 leaf，不再解释为允许全部。项目自有正例锁定显式 key 的 mixed chain，负例锁定空集合为 `dedicated-leaf-unsupported`。因此 `3028090166:111` 的 `Opacity -> Foliage Sway -> Water Waves×4` 不会被 unified owner 错抢；本批也没有提前迁移 Foliage/Water Waves。
- 自动门：现役断言位于 [test_scene_authored_shader_frontend.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_authored_shader_frontend.py)、[test_scene_resolved_material_program_derivation.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_resolved_material_program_derivation.py)、[test_scene_resolved_material_program_finalizer.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_resolved_material_program_finalizer.py)、[test_scene_resolved_material_execution_capability.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_resolved_material_execution_capability.py)、[test_scene_resolved_material_pass_encoder.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_resolved_material_pass_encoder.py)、[test_scene_graph_texture_publication.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_graph_texture_publication.py)、[test_scene_opacity_planner.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_opacity_planner.py)、[test_scene_opacity_rendering.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_opacity_rendering.py)、[test_scene_framebuffer_capture.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_framebuffer_capture.py) 与 [test_scene_wallpaper_benchmark.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_wallpaper_benchmark.py)。它们覆盖 dead sampler/resolution 正反数据流、typed optional mask ready/missing/unproven purpose、Program identity、严格 leaf allowlist、GPU publication/completion、resolved subject 排除旧 catalog，以及历史无图 planner 的像素合同；R4-A+C 后的现役语义门另锁 rejected graph 抑制 legacy plan/inputs。resolved-material GPU 门使用四象限非对称 2×2 纹理逐像素比较，额外锁定 pass 输入行序与输出行序一致，避免垂直镜像被颜色集合断言漏过。
- R4-A+C 前定向证据：`.codex/scene-opacity-r4-2-targeted-20260808-v10/report.json` 中 `3141421197` 恢复 resolved Program/executor、GPU/compositor/next-frame、exact backend 与非黑窗口；其 shader 不采样 `g_Texture0`，但活用 `g_Texture0Resolution` 做坐标，因此 active schema 保留真实 graph resource extent。`.codex/scene-opacity-r4-2-targeted-20260808-v14/report.json` 中 `2902406982:[365,372,647,664]` 由 resolved Program/executor 接管，其他 family 不被本批误迁移。两份报告 SHA-256 分别为 `4983f7734c27ded5886bcd74171528d7fe24f30ab33722f2b94afcb1ae0bd261`、`d796e0437b262460cb879158f7f82be937f2ebf7d774c9c201f0cb625bfc3f85`；它们保留为 Program/方向历史证据，不证明现役 rejected-graph 行为。
- R4-A+C 前 full45 校准证据：`.codex/scene-opacity-r4-2-full45-20260808-v2/report.json` 在刷新当时 owner 合同前为 **36/45 直接 PASS**；另 8 项只发生 owner/stage/disposition/chain 预期失配，均 loaded ratio `1.0`、failed frame `0`、ready/after 非黑、resolved executor failure `0`、graph validation 与 route missing 为空；唯一非 owner 红项仍是 `2067939514` 的 particle candidate/skipped-transparent 既有偏差。报告 SHA-256 `43d06e78aa331b43ea0522ef6d1c9d4ffdcd34fd07d00d7b3ba88e2dd406f189`；据此当时只刷新 8 个 owner 合同，`2067939514` 未变化。该历史结构门不证明任一样本的纹理方向或视觉等价，也已被现役 R4-A+C authority 合同 supersede。
- R4-A+C 前最终 full45：`.codex/scene-opacity-r4-2-milestone-full45-20260808-v1/report.json` **44/45**；唯一失败仍为 `2067939514` 的 `particle candidate count mismatch` 与 `particle skipped transparent count mismatch`，其余 44 个样本 loaded ratio `1.0`、failed frame `0`，没有新增 owner、graph、GPU 或 route 红项。报告 SHA-256 `6c4a0e45bd82cbccdaa938b53ce632eabda2f471e75c5e56d42ba77745695165`，full matrix SHA-256 `579a028dc998cca627a740482ddd5716a004a40d01f82b1482714d984b2b9b5f`，fixed13 suite 文件 SHA-256 `6c9ef8b648d4ba209e50667178c9810dcf209c0b1fa88770039796debd6f0ef5`；固定 suite expand/compact/expand 与 matrix suite/contract **35/35 PASS**。`3088601835` 与 `3743305891` 虽结构 PASS，仍不构成纹理 Y 轴方向已修复的视觉证据。该报告不再是现役 authority/full45 基线；R4-A+C 后 fresh full45 待整合者完成后补入。
- 纹理方向回归闭环：Opacity 迁移后的公共 authored vertex wrapper 曾把同一组 texture UV 直接用于 Metal full-target quad 几何，混淆了纹理 V 轴与 clip 几何 Y 轴，使每次 resolved-material 离屏 pass 垂直镜像一次。现役 wrapper 保持 authored `a_TexCoord` 不变，只对用于 `a_Position` 的几何 Y 做 `1-v` 映射；没有修改 TEX/PNG/视频上传、physical/mapped extent、sampler 或 layer world transform，也没有样本、layer、路径、hash 分支。定向报告 `.codex/scene-texture-orientation-r4-targeted-20260808-v3/report.json` 对 `3088601835` / `3743305891` 为 **2/2 PASS**、loaded ratio 均 `1.0`、failed frame / drawable miss 均 `0`、sample-root residue 为空，报告 SHA-256 `47c76a39eb4ad0e8bcce31af7f0b00fabcd7f3a2be32125483017b87f5ade917`。前者 resolved layer `54` 的 claimed/encoded/GPU 为 `64/64/64`，后者 resolved video layer `23` 为 `6/6/6`，executor failure、graph diagnostic/failed/GPU failed 与 legacy conflict 均为 `0`；人工比较 after 截图与 Opacity 迁移前方向证据，确认前者后帽/兜帽层重新贴合头脸，后者整体人物恢复头朝上。该结果只闭合这两个方向性回归及公共 pass 行序合同，不证明所有 TEX/视频/effect、同相位 Windows 像素或 Wallpaper Engine 等价。
- 保留边界：这批只迁移 fresh 证据覆盖的普通 Opacity Program owner并修复其暴露的统一 pass 方向回归；SceneScript/dynamic alpha、无法完整接管的 masked mixed chain、legacy inline/composition、logical target/multi-pass、未知 sampler/state/color 与 Windows 同步像素/时序 golden 仍未闭合。typed mask variant 可编译/执行不等于包含其他未迁移 leaf 的整条 layer chain 已迁移。R4-2 只完成普通 Opacity 子集，R4 仍未完成，R5 未准入。

<a id="e-effect-color-key"></a>
### E-EFFECT-COLOR-KEY: stock-equivalent Color Key 与 relocated 作者副本

- 实现提交：准入 `ee69fc4`，premultiplied-alpha 修正 `0fb0669`；该 dedicated owner 已在 R4-2 mixed graph 波次撤权，当前执行由统一 Program/executor 接管。现役入口为 [SceneResolvedMaterialExecutionCapability.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/EffectExecution/SceneResolvedMaterialExecutionCapability.swift)、[SceneResolvedMaterialGraphExecutor.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/EffectExecution/SceneResolvedMaterialGraphExecutor.swift)。
- 准入合同：definition 的 replacement key、单 pass 形状和依赖必须闭合到同一 material/shader identity；material/instance/resolved 三层都要求零纹理槽、只允许 INVERT/FLATTEN、normal/nocull/depth disabled。vertex/fragment 路径从作者 identity 推导，raw SHA 必须与实际 source 自洽，可执行源码去掉逐行注释后必须与已验证 stock 2.8.42 指纹相同。因此路径重定位、material JSON 字段顺序和 annotation JSON 键顺序不影响准入，任一可执行 token、texture slot、combo、常量或 render state 改动仍失败关闭；没有 workshop ID、layer ID 或样本 ID 分支。
- 自动门：旧 Color Key dedicated planner/rendering fixtures 已随 owner 撤权删除；当前由统一 graph executor 的 stage identity、Program finalizer 和 mixed-chain 正反门覆盖，benchmark 只保留历史 Color Key 语料证据，不再把旧 dedicated count 当作现役 owner 门。
- 现役运行门：`.codex/scene-chain-r4-colorkey-mixed-targeted-20260808-v2/report.json` 对 `3767460992` **1/1 PASS**，报告 SHA-256 `3ba289c14169569d8482bf9f1df3d2098002970bf4fec530d7949a31d513c4b9`；resolved `[20,945,994]` accepted/claimed/encoded/GPU/compositor/next-frame 集合闭合，exact backend 全为 `resolved-material-graph`，legacy conflict、graph diagnostic/failed outcome 与 executor failure 均为 0。fresh full45 `.codex/scene-chain-r4-colorkey-mixed-full45-20260808-v3/report.json` 为 **44/45，非 PASS**，唯一失败仍是 `2067939514` 的 particle candidate/skipped-transparent 既有偏差；报告/full matrix SHA-256 为 `734dc558e9e7f38f66d3c7b6f650b26e58bd2c0a1c272c141e3eeef122b01b48` / `f1b4912475f27ff15220e6fae11d24d02e495b49816a15638c71adb687cb427f`。四个 resolved 样本共 6 层 / 7 stage，executor claimed/encoded/GPU `247/247/247`、failure `0`；App executable SHA-256 `6177827d02f2c8803aab331eb246631da7ecce0d3a4690526159c048a5bd78c6`、CDHash `654b76282a794409e3b3821dc0a3f7928405163b`、Team `H9QWU9XN8R`，签名前后 verified。
- 视觉边界：用户提供的官方客户端截图与当前原生 3024x1964 帧都保留中心 `X + 半透明矩形` 的无音频唱片盒设计；放射圆点属于 Audio Bars 并随系统音频输入变化，不是贴图黑点。两次帧未锁定同一场景时刻、音频输入和作者属性，不能作为像素 golden。当前只执行静态 alpha/fuzziness/tolerance/color 与 INVERT/FLATTEN；dynamic binding、纹理输入、未知 shader/state/combo、非等价 Workshop variants 和 Windows 同步像素门仍未闭合。

<a id="e-audio-input"></a>
### E-AUDIO-INPUT: Scene 16/32/64 频段音频频谱输入管线

- 实现提交：底座 `eccb36a`，求值器 `72420f0`，接入 surface 求值链路与 consumer 驱动 `72ad176`，32/64 Workshop 档位 `715e078`，可重定位 16-band stereo consumer `e315743`；`9a6a028`的两个fixed native 64-band property-script consumer已由B21删除。现役代码：[SceneAudioSpectrum.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Runtime/SceneAudioSpectrum.swift)、[SceneAudioResponse.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Runtime/SceneAudioResponse.swift)、[SystemAudioSceneSpectrumAnalyzer.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/Playback/SystemAudioSceneSpectrumAnalyzer.swift)、[SceneDesktopWallpaperHost+AudioDemand.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Runtime/SceneDesktopWallpaperHost+AudioDemand.swift)。
- 自动门：[test_scene_audio_spectrum_input.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_audio_spectrum_input.py) 覆盖三档snapshot形状、非法值归零、inbox代际与需求生命周期、静音稳定零输入、低到高顺序、同输入同输出、非法采样率/空输入fail closed，以及同一次左右声道FFT同时产生16/32/64档；[test_scene_audio_response.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_audio_response.py)锁stock 16档求值；[test_scene_audio_demand.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_audio_demand.py)锁consumer驱动、每帧单次采样广播与Program/dedicated Effect Audio Bars需求接线。Simple Audio Bars继续由公共[test_scene_authored_shader_frontend.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_authored_shader_frontend.py)、[test_scene_resolved_material_pass_encoder.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_resolved_material_pass_encoder.py)与[test_scene_resolved_material_graph_executor.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_resolved_material_graph_executor.py)锁作者数组、Program与GPU编码边界；三个fixed SceneScript Audio Bars专用测试已随B21产品面删除。
- 官方合同来源：stock `shake.vert` / `pulse.vert` 的 `CreateAudioResponse` 逐字一致（测试锁定该事实）。三处按原样保留：官方算出 `audioFrequencyEnd = max(min, max)` 后从未使用；除数是未经 `max()` 修正的 `frequencyMax - frequencyMin + 1`，`min > max` 时循环不执行、除数为负，结果是 `smoothstep(bounds, 0)` 而非非法输入；`saturate` 只作用于 `pow` 结果，`multiply` 在其后相乘，因此响应可大于 1。
- 公开作者合同：[IEngine.registerAudioBuffers](https://docs.wallpaperengine.io/en/scene/scenescript/reference/class/IEngine.html) 只接受 16/32/64，[AudioBuffers](https://docs.wallpaperengine.io/en/scene/scenescript/reference/class/AudioBuffers.html) 的等长 left/right/average 数组每帧更新、从低频排到高频且通常 0...1 但允许超过 1；[shader audio globals](https://docs.wallpaperengine.io/en/scene/shader/variables.html) 也明确三档左右声道为正值且不归一化。当前新 16-band profile 因而不对峰值作 1.0 上限裁剪；这不意味着项目 FFT 的频段映射或数值尺度与 Windows 相同。
- 边界：host snapshot现承载16/32/64档left/right；stock effect与[E-WORKSHOP-SIMPLE-AUDIO-BARS](#e-workshop-simple-audio-bars)的strict relocated stereo up/down profile消费16档，另两个exact bottom profile消费32/64档。B21删除fixed native property-script的逐bin average geometry consumer；项目不创建官方`AudioBuffers` object、Float32Array identity、脚本订阅或通用average桥。其他Workshop shader与通用SceneScript`registerAudioBuffers`仍未接入。频段划分（32 Hz→16 kHz对数）、-60 dB归一化和平滑是本项目工程选择，官方未公开，**不构成数值parity**；与Web侧64+64是两套独立合同。采集30 Hz、渲染60 Hz，不插值，同一快照可在两帧间重复使用并带generation。
- 生命周期：采集由consumer存在性驱动（stock Shake/Pulse、普通/Workshop Effect Audio Bars的Program或typed plan，以及已准入particle consumer），launch时声明、teardown时撤销；`playbackPaused`/`screenLocked`/`systemSleeping`/`displaysSleeping`沿用既有`captureAllowed`，不采集时立即归零。B21已从demand移除fixed SceneScript Audio Bars program。macOS <14.2、无权限或设备失败输出稳定全零并出诊断，不产生假波形。

<a id="e-scenescript-audio-bars"></a>
### E-SCENESCRIPT-AUDIO-BARS: fixed native profiles retired

- B21现役边界：`SceneScriptBindingDefinition`仍保真layer property wrapper的host/source/properties/authored fallback，但没有fixed Audio Bars compiler、plan、verification、native geometry/renderer或产品route。任何相同或变异source都不会因SHA、Workshop/model/material路径、property shape或shader指纹取得64-draw产品执行权。
- 历史合同：`9a6a028`曾让两个exact profile以64/64/`average`、同纹理64 draw执行，并由三个专用测试与benchmark字段约束。B21已删除这些产品类型、专用测试、host report/telemetry及benchmark metrics；旧合同只说明撤权前形态，不能作为现役fallback或oracle链接。
- 历史运行边界：`.codex/scene-script-audio-bars-targeted-20260730-v2/report.json`对`2241938645`/`3743305891`曾为 **2/2 PASS**，但系统输入为静音；该报告及其plan/diagnostic/bar/GPU计数只描述B21前二进制，不证明现役owner、当前输出、非零条形或视觉。通用VM、`registerAudioBuffers`、`AudioBuffers`、`ILayer`/`IScene` handles继续为`L0`。

<a id="e-workshop-simple-audio-bars"></a>
### E-WORKSHOP-SIMPLE-AUDIO-BARS: exact 2084198056 bottom 与 relocated stereo up/down profiles

- 原32/64 bottom专用实现提交为`715e078`；严格可重定位16-band profile与Fisheye ordered follow-up为`e315743`。2026-08-09先后把Program-compatible solid、无child/dependency composition与relocated 16-band `Program -> Fisheye zero-distortion`完整链交给同一executor。本批在三个精确旧profile均已有Program正例后，删除Simple Audio Bars专用planner/profile、Metal pipeline、renderer dispatch与只服务旧链的测试；增强版 Audio Bars仍保留自己的`.workshopAudioBars` backend，不能把runtime backend计数误写成Simple family未撤权。composition继续通过有界main-target source route在作者层触发点消费此前已绘制的framebuffer，Fisheye仍作为统一链strict dedicated leaf顺序执行。代码：[SceneResolvedMaterialExecutionCapability.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/EffectExecution/SceneResolvedMaterialExecutionCapability.swift)、[SceneResolvedMaterialGraphExecutor.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/EffectExecution/SceneResolvedMaterialGraphExecutor.swift)、[SceneResolvedMaterialFramePreflight.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Rendering/SceneResolvedMaterialFramePreflight.swift)、[SceneAuthoredShaderStraightBlendOutputAnalyzer.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/ShaderFrontend/SceneAuthoredShaderStraightBlendOutputAnalyzer.swift)、[SceneAuthoredShaderVectorConversion.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/ShaderFrontend/SceneAuthoredShaderVectorConversion.swift)。
- 现役准入合同不再按`2084198056`路径、relocation结构、raw/canonical SHA或三个combo tuple选择专用算法；统一Program只在graph/material/source/variant/frontend/reflection/uniform/render-state/color与source route完整可静态证明时接管。已证语料仍是original `32+CLIP_LOW`、original `64+CLIP_HIGH`与relocated `RESOLUTION=16, SHAPE=9, ANTIALIAS=1, BLENDMODE=31, TRANSPARENCY=4`三种形态；其他shape、透明/混合、AA、topology若不能形成完整Program便整链失败关闭，不再回退到Simple专用近似。`2938612768:563`实际省略`RESOLUTION`并使用默认32档；此前文档称其为64-band是事实错误，现已更正。没有sample/layer ID算法分支。
- 参数与GPU：original profile保留slot-origin相邻band插值、居中spacing、bottom bounds/clip与replace premultiplied输出。Program路径不按effect/path/hash重写算法：frontend直接编译作者shader；`material:"previous"`经typed graph-input alias绑定当前effect input。公共color analyzer除原单sample normal/straight-alpha外，只在helper首个root return精确为`A + B * opacity`、调用为`mix(color.rgb, sample.rgb, sample.a)`、alpha精确为`sample.a * 同一weight`且读写守恒时证明additive/intersect straight输出；其他表达继续`colorContractUnproven`。作者语料中的`uint x = floatValue % scalar`只在简单标量赋值、两侧类型静态可证且至少一侧为float时显式翻译为`fmod`后再构造目标整数；compound/vector/歧义形式继续交给Metal失败关闭。Program variant内实际出现16/32/64左右host uniform时驱动audio demand；最终premultiply不在乘alpha前夹RGB，避免additive高光被提前截断，UNORM target仍负责最终存储范围。relocated 16-band当前由作者Program执行left/right上下条、AA、source-alpha intersect与additive输出，随后strict zero-distortion Fisheye按原顺序消费其结果；`Bar Color`与`Opacity`继续走typed snapshot consumer。
- 运行门：历史`.codex/scene-simple-audio-bars-targeted-algo-final-20260728/report.json`为original两个专用profile **2/2 PASS**，只保留为迁移前oracle。各自Program迁移报告随后证明ordinary `3122339805:64`、composition `2938612768:563`与relocated `3299228616:151`。删除专用owner后的现役家族门`.codex/scene-simple-audio-bars-owner-retirement-r4-2-20260809-v1/report.json`为**3/3 PASS**，报告/临时matrix SHA-256 `ecc320b016d461a487778190a913c9f9e7962f3da1b04532abba67ea8ebe4211` / `03e0e0361fcf03aa847163b0989bbc4cdd53601e5462c15b211061a4e13166c5`。三样本resolved集合依次`[390,563]`、`[64,324,466,495,542,546,571,579,628,637,668,675]`、`[151,303,601]`，executor claimed/encoded/GPU依次`148/148/148`、`996/996/996`、`159/159/159`，failure均0；成功transaction `6/24/10`，terminal/compositor/next-frame覆盖全部resolved layer，graph diagnostic/failed outcome/GPU failure、failed frame与drawable miss均0，旧Workshop Simple Audio Bars计数均0。签名App `2.0.8 (268)`、Team `H9QWU9XN8R`、executable SHA-256 `af7c5704f6a3b13f1ed0dbea9f84a3536ffc82f3afbdc58bdc88deb772b83c99`、CDHash `4a93f72aa143350954939d2a4ffad94e8687f857`，签名前后verified。Fisheye仍是统一链内dedicated leaf，不冒充generic Program；增强版Audio Bars仍是独立bounded family。正式full45矩阵保持未改，不能把旧matrix ratchet误写成产品回归。
- 视觉与回归边界：删除owner后的家族门ready/after均非黑；`2938612768`底部默认32档黑条从短横线增长为15根独立竖柱，`3122339805`顶部64档紫条由低幅短柱增长为高幅柱，`3299228616`上排16档向上、下排向下且条间独立，没有整块黑矩形、泛白或整层染色。fixture是确定性验证输入，不是官方FFT数值或同步Windows音源；均匀频段只验证高度/方向/颜色/spacing，不验证真实频谱的逐bin形状。其他revision/shape/transparency/blend/AA、非零Fisheye、Scroll/SceneScript sibling、partial chain、带child/dependency的composition与未证project/fullscreen继续fail closed；无Windows像素golden，不宣称WE parity。

<a id="e-audio-effect"></a>
### E-AUDIO-EFFECT: stock/old-editor Shake 与 stock Pulse 的 AUDIOPROCESSING 执行

- 实现提交：stock Shake `72ad176`，Pulse 与共享准入层 `5451cb1`，old-editor Shake audio `f26aae5`。代码：[SceneAudioResponseAdmission.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/SceneAudioResponseAdmission.swift)、[SceneAuthoredShakePlanner+Audio.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/SceneAuthoredShakePlanner+Audio.swift)、[SceneAuthoredPulsePlanner+Constants.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/SceneAuthoredPulsePlanner+Constants.swift)。
- 官方分支语义：Shake 的时间驱动整段包在 `#if AUDIOPROCESSING == 0` 内，启用后 speed/friction/bounds/flowPhase 均不参与，`DIRECTION == 0` 下 `offset = v_AudioPulse` 再乘 `strength² * flowMask`；Pulse 先无条件 `pulse = v_Pulse`，再由 `#if AUDIOPROCESSING == 0` 分支整体覆盖，启用后 speed/phase/amount/bounds/noise/power 均不参与。两者的项目实现均在 CPU 侧按帧求出标量随 uniform 上传，与官方 `varying`（每 draw 常量）等价而非同址实现。
- 常量默认值：五个 audio 常量在语料中部分可选（`2419444134` 缺 frequencymin/max、`1937925563` 还缺 audioexponent），缺省按 shader annotation 回填。`audiobounds` 默认值两 effect 不同——shake `0.0 1.2`、pulse `0.5 1.0`——由各 planner 传入，共享层不内置（测试锁定）。
- profile 准入：Shake 为 `whitePhaseFallback`/`timeOffsetCombo` 与 exact `legacyUnconditionalPhase`，Pulse 仅 `stock2842`。legacy Shake 只接受完整已登记 shader profile、`DIRECTION=0`、`AUDIOPROCESSING=1...3` 与既有 audio 常量边界；省略 phase 或显式 `util/white` 均归一为内置 R8 白相位。
- 自动门：[test_scene_shake_planner.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_shake_planner.py)、[test_scene_shake_rendering.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_shake_rendering.py)、[test_scene_audio_demand.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_audio_demand.py) 与 Pulse 既有门覆盖常量默认、old-editor definition、profile/slot/combo 变异、静音与非零 pulse；注入非零 pulse 时 GPU 输出相对静音改变超过 20 个像素，NaN 输入失败关闭。
- 边界：其余 legacy 指纹、Shake `DIRECTION` 1/2、material 层 audio combo、未启用 audio 却声明 audio 常量和其他 Workshop shader 继续拒绝。真实 benchmark 使用稳定静音，只证明 runtime chain 与零输入；尚无同步非零系统音频截图或 Windows 数值/像素 golden。

<a id="e-effect-shake"></a>
### E-EFFECT-SHAKE: exact stock / old-editor flow-map Shake profile

- 实现提交：stock `e505a9e`，old-editor definition/audio `f26aae5`，exact slot 3 opacity mask `1cbc1451`，三槽 slot-binding/sampler 迁移 `6665cb98`。代码：[SceneAuthoredShakePlanner.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/SceneAuthoredShakePlanner.swift)、[SceneShakeTextureAdmission.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/SceneShakeTextureAdmission.swift)、[SceneShakeEffectTextureLoader.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Resources/SceneShakeEffectTextureLoader.swift)、[SceneTextureSlotBinding.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Resources/SceneTextureSlotBinding.swift)、[SceneShakePipeline.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Effects/SceneShakePipeline.swift)、[SceneShakeRenderer.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Effects/SceneShakeRenderer.swift)、[SceneEffectStageExecutionPlan+Backend.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/EffectCompilation/SceneEffectStageExecutionPlan+Backend.swift)。
- 自动门：[test_scene_texture_candidate.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_texture_candidate.py)、[test_scene_effect_instance_texture_loader.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_effect_instance_texture_loader.py)、[test_scene_shake_planner.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_shake_planner.py)、[test_scene_shake_rendering.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_shake_rendering.py)、[test_scene_effect_texture_purposes.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_effect_texture_purposes.py)、[test_scene_resolved_material_execution_capability.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_resolved_material_execution_capability.py)、[test_scene_wallpaper_benchmark.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_wallpaper_benchmark.py)。覆盖 exact definition/material/raw shader fingerprint、slot 1 RG8/RGBA flow、slot 2 R8 phase 或 authored white fallback、slot 3 mask、scene time、Blur 前后顺序、逐槽 purpose/format/path/UV/sampler、mask 限定位移区域，以及未知第五槽、路径错配、资源缺失、错误 device、未知 fingerprint、dynamic audio/speed 与各槽 clamp-border 失败关闭。
- 运行门：现役 slot-binding 定向门见页首，六样本 Shake 合计 33、failed layers 空。`3738202317:16` 为 chain/stage `1/4`、Shake/Pulse/Water Ripple `2/1/1`；`1937925563` 的 12 个 `legacyUnconditionalPhase`、`AUDIOPROCESSING=3` Shake 全部执行；`2802243144` 的 bird layers `[41,64,115]` 为 3 chain / 6 stage / Shake 3，人物 layer 58 仍因 user-bound `speed` 阻断；`2998757800` 的 16 个重复 Shake 共同执行。六组 ready/after 均非黑且有运动，人工未见黑帧、单侧遮罩丢失、异常裁切或粗大 mask 伪影；preview/官方截图不能证明精确相位或 pixel parity。
- 边界：只接受单 pass 显式 `MASK=0/NOISETEXTURE=0`、三个 exact shader profile、已验证纹理槽与 `DIRECTION=0`；slot 1 flow 只接受已证 axis-aligned mapped UV/format，slot 2 phase 必须 R8 且 UV scale 与 flow 相同，slot 3 mask 接受已证 axis-aligned mapped UV；三槽 clamp-border、rotation/translation 均拒绝。可选 slot 3 必须是唯一 instance asset，槽存在才执行 inferred mask 分支；显式 `MASK=1`、dynamic generation/speed/noise/direction、unknown shader/combo/state 和 unsupported sibling 继续整链失败关闭。当前执行项目内 MSL，不编译 authored shader，也没有 Windows pixel golden。
- legacy 指纹：`legacyUnconditionalPhase`（frag `c4911d58…`、canonical `af9b4c97…`）允许老编辑器省略 `replacementkey`，但仍要求 version/name/group/pass/material/shader 全部精确；phase 可省略或显式 `util/white`，flow 只接受已验证 RG88/ARGB8888。`1636394814`/`1553008362` 的缺 version、label-as-key 常量等其他差异仍失败关闭。

- Blend 边界：只接受单 pass、无 authored RT、slot 1 单纹理、`BLENDMODE=0`、`NUMBLENDTEXTURES=1`、`TRANSFORMUV=0`、`TRANSFORMREPEAT=0`、`WRITEALPHA=0/1`、`OPACITYMASK=0`、中性 angle/offset/scale。`WRITEALPHA=0` 要求 alpha=1 并保持 source alpha；`WRITEALPHA=1` 只接受静态有限 alpha 0...1，输出 alpha 为 overlay alpha × authored alpha，RGB 先在 straight space 混合再按输出 alpha 预乘。静态有限 multiply 与 exact `script,user,value` wrapper 下的有界 time-of-day 算术可执行；脚本只准 documented `engine.timeOfDay`、`WEMath.smoothStep`、`Math.max`、常数与有限四则运算，完整语法匹配后才发布 typed `.effectConstant(..., "multiply")` target，运行时按本地系统时区的民用日秒归一化并复用同一帧 wall date。官方 [Blend effect](https://docs.wallpaperengine.io/en/scene/effects/effect/blend.html) 只公开 Write alpha 会按 blended texture opacity 修改底层不透明度；[IEngine](https://docs.wallpaperengine.io/en/scene/scenescript/reference/class/IEngine.html) 把 `timeOfDay` 定义为本地 24 小时 `[0,1]`。项目的精确准入与 premultiplication 合同来自合法 stock 语料和自有正反门，不冒充未公开的 Windows 实现。普通 property 图片与 authored asset 均形成 premultiplied-color typed candidate；最终 binding 必须是 slot 1、RGBA/BGRA、axis-aligned mapped UV、非 clamp-border。其他 blend mode、多纹理、T2-T6、effect-combo UV transform/repeat、opacity mask、动态 alpha、named RT/media、其他动态 target/语法/调用和未知指纹继续拒绝。
- Transform 边界：只接受 stock 2.8.42 单 material、无 authored RT、`MODE=1`、`CLAMP=1` 且 `angle=0`、`offset=(0,0)`、`scale=(1,1)` 的 identity profile；执行复用 framebuffer capture/copy，不实现 transform 数学。`2067939514` 两个 scale 的 `valueKind=binding` 没有可执行 user/timeline binding，fallback components 均为 `[1,1]`，因此只按静态 identity fallback 执行并逐项输出 `unsupported-dynamic-binding-static-fallback`；这不代表 dynamic scale 或 live update 已支持。非 identity center/offset/rotation/scale、repeat/`CLAMP=0`、Vertex/UV variants 与未知 hash 继续拒绝。
- 运行门：现役 write-alpha 聚焦门为隔离 `2134765860` 的 `.codex/scene-blend-writealpha-2134765860-20260802-day-v2/report.json` 与 `.codex/scene-blend-writealpha-2134765860-20260802-night-v2/report.json`，分别注入本地 12:00 / 06:00，均 **1/1 PASS**、binding 3、succeeded `[246,301]`、Blend/chain/stage `4/2/5`、failed frame/drawable miss 0。跨样本 `.codex/scene-blend-writealpha-cross-sample-2938612768-20260802-v1/report.json` 保持 Blend 0，证明 named/dynamic 形态未被误准入。完整 `.codex/scene-blend-writealpha-full45-20260802-v1/report.json` 为 **45/45 PASS**，报告/full matrix SHA-256 `3ccdd383ab5b859d6eca2afcd2df42349d00968fe458c0d37d4314f6e3a78b4e` / `7265932936ee54d5fa97605f02a02b60635e63e388dd5e0f56b9fb6b175eaac3`；fixed13 本批未跑。旧 time-of-day 与 `2067939514` 报告仍是该早期子能力的历史证据，不覆盖当前基线。
- Program owner运行门：`.codex/scene-blend-r4-2-2067939514-20260809-v3/report.json`对`2067939514`为**1/1 PASS**，报告SHA-256 `94c94ba0f67a0bc2f0ff72d9d106939d78e82c95de3d7f4a470bcb90fbf21a10`。layer`342`是唯一accepted resolved layer，stage为`admitted-generic / resolved-material / program`，executor claimed/encoded/GPU为`100/100/100`、failure 0；两次terminal success与两次成功transaction均被compositor和next-frame消费，graph diagnostic/failed outcome/GPU failure/failed frame为0，ready/after非黑，局部星点变化而主体方向、圆形遮罩和Windows标志保持稳定。首次按现役正式单样本matrix运行的v2只因Blend owner/count预期迁移及`2067939514`既有particle ratchet而非PASS，报告SHA-256 `9b9b99e6493f666c308864d975b94e20c6a18a4e8ff12781a2dd769d9765f9b2`并保留为失败现场；v3使用临时定向matrix只移除无关particle ratchet且不改正式矩阵。本批未跑fixed13/full45。签名App `2.0.8 (268)`、Team `H9QWU9XN8R`、executable SHA-256 `b66f8f355bb7166f2bb42849402562069659387a99e49930871621b2bccc6429`、CDHash `6ffc2f051a036a5abdaf5b55b5797102ae6628fd`，签名前后verified。

### E-EFFECT-EXECUTION-EVIDENCE: admission、disposition 与实际编码分轴证据

- 代码：[SceneEffectStageAdmission.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/EffectCompilation/SceneEffectStageAdmission.swift)、[SceneEffectAdmissionCatalog.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/EffectCompilation/SceneEffectAdmissionCatalog.swift)、[SceneEffectRuntimeDispositionCatalog.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Runtime/SceneEffectRuntimeDispositionCatalog.swift)、[SceneEffectExecutionTelemetry.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Runtime/SceneEffectExecutionTelemetry.swift)、[SceneEffectExecutionFrameTrace.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Runtime/SceneEffectExecutionFrameTrace.swift)、[SceneGPUCompletionTelemetry.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Runtime/SceneGPUCompletionTelemetry.swift)、[SceneMetalRenderer+EffectExecution.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Rendering/SceneMetalRenderer+EffectExecution.swift)、[scene_matrix_contract.py](../../../script/scene_matrix_contract.py) 与 [scene_wallpaper_benchmark.py](../../../script/scene_wallpaper_benchmark.py)。生产选择不含 sample ID、layer ID、资源 hash 或 mask 名分支。
- 合同：descriptor admission 逐条保留 activity、strict coverage、backend/profile 与稳定拒绝原因；static runtime disposition 再把每条 `EffectKey` 唯一归到 strict/legacy/structural/shadowed/route-only/composite-refused/unsupported/inactive 等状态。帧内 exact subject 使用 `layerID + effectIndex + descriptorID`，真实 coalesced legacy 只允许 `layer + family` aggregate；CPU invocation、route operation 与 shared-frame command-buffer status 独立记录 sticky success/failure。共享 command buffer 完成只说明包含该 cohort 的提交完成，不推导逐 stage GPU 成功。
- demand 与矩阵：同代、schema/validation 有效的 disposition 只有在 exact eligible、aggregate subject 或 direct/authored/legacy-offscreen/offscreen-passthrough/composite-refused route group 存在时才要求 execution evidence；空 effect/纯 inactive 可合法无日志。missing/invalid disposition、未知 group kind、七字段部分存在或 evidence 漂移全部失败关闭。矩阵只注册 schema 与 exact/aggregate eligible-observed-gap 七个跨运行稳定字段；frame ID、frame 数组、canonical SHA 与尚未承诺稳定的 transition count 不进入基线。
- 自动门：[test_scene_effect_execution_telemetry.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_effect_execution_telemetry.py)、[test_scene_authored_effect_execution.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_authored_effect_execution.py)、[test_scene_resolved_material_execution_capability.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_resolved_material_execution_capability.py)、[test_scene_authored_effect_execution.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_authored_effect_execution.py)、[test_scene_matrix_contract.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_matrix_contract.py) 与 [test_scene_wallpaper_benchmark.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_wallpaper_benchmark.py)。最终 benchmark/matrix 合同 117/117；Scene 全量 994 tests（11 skipped）；Swift code health 为 767 files / 44 locked legacy / 400-line limit，签名 Debug `script/build_and_run.sh verify` 通过。
- 真实门：首轮 `.codex/scene-chain-r0c-full45-20260803-v1/report.json` 保留 **42/45，FAIL**，分别暴露独立 particle 偏差、合法 no-demand 误判与 X-Ray 灰屏；不能覆盖。公共 demand 与 X-Ray 纹理角色修复后，`.codex/scene-chain-r0c-full45-20260803-v2/report.json` 为 **44/45，overall FAIL**，报告 SHA-256 `219036a306a629d76876894b0e318b42d1c0b53dc9750f7be28618ceca5f2219`，唯一红项为 `2067939514` 的独立 particle baseline 偏差。R0 scoped refresh 得到 full matrix SHA-256 `6ba6f188a467669b139a29cd135ea0603c0cafa9fadfa370384ec44739351072`；刷新后正式 consumer 门 `.codex/scene-chain-r0c-post-refresh-20260803-v1/report.json` 为 3/3。
- 边界：eligible 是静态可执行 subject 集，不保证给定运行窗口一定调度；observed gap 必须保留，不能伪造 invocation 消除。本证据只回答“声明如何准入、归属、被哪个真实编码点调用并进入哪类共享提交”，不证明 shader 数值、局部 ROI、跨相位视觉、长期性能或 Wallpaper Engine parity。

<a id="e-graph-r8-target"></a>
### E-GRAPH-R8-TARGET: authored R8 Metal-storage target foundation

- 证据与实现边界：官方2.8.42 stock `assets/effects/glitter/effect.json`（本机官方客户端固定副本SHA-256 `f7d4fda31b42a0e22532bbed2ac2592b03c3fca4a3f70f17563b31e96c44288a`）的29-37行声明固定256×256、`format:r8`、`uvs:repeat`的单FBO，8-27行声明prepare写该target、combine再绑定`previous`与该target；`glitter_prepare.frag:30-34`的fragment output把同一信号写入RGB，R8 attachment因而只持久化red，`glitter_combine.frag:7,29`把该target作为slot 1并显式读取`.r`。该stock material/shader未出现`TEX0FORMAT`、`TEX1FORMAT`或`FORMAT_R8`条件，已观察的variant条件只有`BLENDMODE`与可选`MASK`，所以这里只把`r8`认定为target storage/channel合同，不能据此授予format-driven shader-variant owner。Xcode 26.0本机macOS SDK的`MTLPixelFormat.h`把`MTLPixelFormatR8Unorm`列为normal 8-bit format。项目据此只建立clean-room Metal storage映射`r8 -> .r8Unorm`和1 logical B/texel记账，不把Metal枚举冒充Wallpaper Engine公开backend合同，也不复制stock shader/payload/算法表达；该底层决策已有官方stock结构与平台类型证据，本批没有启动Ghidra。
- 代码链：[SceneGraphRenderTargetPlan.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/GraphTargets/SceneGraphRenderTargetPlan.swift)与[extent/format admission](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/GraphTargets/SceneGraphRenderTargetPlan+Extent.swift)只让`r8`进入新增typed case，[SceneGraphRenderTargetFormat.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/GraphTargets/SceneGraphRenderTargetFormat.swift)集中定义logical byte cost与Metal mapping；[target table](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/GraphTargets/SceneGraphRenderTargetTable.swift)、[persistent allocator](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/GraphTargets/ScenePersistentGraphTargetAllocator.swift)、[lease](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/GraphTargets/SceneGraphRenderTargetLease.swift)与[history residency](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/GraphTargets/SceneOffscreenTextureResidency.swift)共用该格式合同。在`e0187153` storage批次提交时，只有[resource clear/copy](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/EffectExecution/SceneGraphResourcePassEncoder.swift)接纳`.r8Unorm`且copy继续要求同extent/format；普通Program attachment只接受BGRA/RGBA，[graph publication](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/GraphTargets/SceneGraphRenderTargetLease+Publication.swift)对R8返回`storageSemanticUnavailable`。FBO storage没有写入TEX-container专属`authoredFormat`，因此该批没有给R8取得Program attachment/publication或TEX-format variant fact；后续bounded scalar-red原子见[E-GRAPH-R8-SCALAR-RED](#e-graph-r8-scalar-red)。
- 自动/GPU门：受影响九模块合计 **83/83**，其中[target plan](../../../script/tests/test_scene_graph_render_target_plan.py) **16/16**、[Metal target table](../../../script/tests/test_scene_graph_render_target_table.py) **9/9**、[publication](../../../script/tests/test_scene_graph_texture_publication.py) **2/2**、[persistent pool](../../../script/tests/test_scene_offscreen_texture_pool.py) **18/18**、resource pass/Program target/GraphExecutor各1/1、framebuffer **29/29**、Local Contrast **6/6**。正门锁定R8 typed allocation、1 B/texel current/history预算、retire→rehydrate账本与R8 exact-format clear/copy；负门锁定R8普通Program target拒绝和`storageSemanticUnavailable` publication。最终Scene全量为 **174 modules / 130.3 s / ALL OK**，code health为 **819 Swift / 44 locked legacy / 400行**，`script/build_and_run.sh verify`为`BUILD SUCCEEDED`且Developer ID签名有效；`.codex`审计为6.91 GiB、stale candidates 0项。
- 定向运行门：最终签名Debug App对隔离full-matrix样本`3765760121`运行`/private/tmp/mwx-r8-storage-final.QmEszv/run-corrected/report.json`，报告SHA-256 `afbf957b3a9a52ae0f4d47249fae65cc354f344a848519ede65e7ecbf7a8490f`，结果为 **0/1 NON-PASS**。App正常退出且未超时，loaded ratio 1，ready/after非黑、changed ratio `0.075539`，101 submitted/completed、failed frame与drawable miss均为0；既有resolved layers `[68,74,76,82,187]`累计claimed/encoded/GPU `35/35/35`且failure 0，12个transaction的GPU/compositor/next-frame集合完整。16项失败全部来自full matrix仍期待R5已删除的legacy `authored_effect_graph_*` aggregate，而当前runtime为missing；没有改矩阵或放宽门。App executable SHA-256 `1c183d8d843068b96baacfb8042b8bbea6875a6a2c50d6baf6e29b0e6c9ef213`、CDHash `ff52e54510c01d18bc5db505805f07e38654087c`、Team `H9QWU9XN8R`，签名前后verified，sample-root residue为空。失败现场保留；本门不计PASS，也不替换B22 formal full45/fixed13。
- 能力/视觉边界：`3765760121:73`的Glitter在本次author状态为`layer-hidden / inactive`，所以整景非黑、preview montage与其他五层GPU成功只能排除明显整景回归，不能证明R8 storage被产品使用，更不能证明Glitter。该storage批次提交时，Program producer、graph publication、single-channel layout/default GBA与red-only consumer尚未闭合；后续仅bounded Metal scalar-red原子见[E-GRAPH-R8-SCALAR-RED](#e-graph-r8-scalar-red)。Generic FBO家族整体仍保持`L2`；FBO repeat sampler、Glitter两pass与tile动态、authored R8 initialization/load/store/reset、Windows像素/时序golden、`rg88`/`r16f`/`rg1616f`仍未闭合。1 logical B/texel不是`MTLDevice.heapTextureSizeAndAlign`意义的物理驻留字节。

<a id="e-graph-r8-scalar-red"></a>
### E-GRAPH-R8-SCALAR-RED: generic R8 scalar-red Program atom

- 证据与 clean-room 边界：沿用[E-GRAPH-R8-TARGET](#e-graph-r8-target)已经界定的官方2.8.42 stock Glitter结构证据：prepare material写固定R8 target，combine material再显式读取该target的`.r`；Apple平台只为本实现提供`.r8Unorm` Metal storage合同。stock material/shader没有出现`TEX0FORMAT`、`TEX1FORMAT`或`FORMAT_R8`条件，因此本批不授予format-driven variant owner，不把Metal采样缺省G/B/A变成graph语义，也不复制stock payload、shader或算法表达。现有文档、stock corpus与平台合同已经足以界定本原子，本批没有启动Ghidra。
- 代码链：[attachment kind](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/EffectExecution/SceneResolvedMaterialAttachmentKind.swift)由[capability stage admission](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/EffectExecution/SceneResolvedMaterialExecutionCapability+Stages.swift)从typed target descriptor生成，并随[MaterialCapability](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/EffectExecution/SceneResolvedMaterialExecutionCapability+Material.swift)携带到[GraphExecutor attachment gate](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/EffectExecution/SceneResolvedMaterialGraphExecutor+Attachment.swift)，executor不再从pixel format重新猜语义。[attachment storage](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/EffectExecution/SceneResolvedMaterialAttachmentStorage.swift)把已解析四分量fragment output映射为`.scalarRedUnorm`并让R8 pipeline只写`.red`；[lease publication](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/GraphTargets/SceneGraphRenderTargetLease+Publication.swift)发布`.scalarRedUnorm / .preservedChannels / .r8Unorm / authoredFormat=nil`。[channel analyzer](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/ShaderFrontend/SceneAuthoredShaderTextureChannelAnalyzer.swift)只证明active fragment中的immediate `texSample2D/texture2D(...).r`，Program derivation、identity、resolver与slot binding再次核对该fact；variant envelope要求至少一个consumer，且所有实际消费该slot的已编译variant均为`.redOnly`。capability只开放`uvs:nil`、无condition/clear、非unique、恰好一个material writer、至少一个later reader、无copy/swap引用的完整原子。
- 自动/GPU门：前端、Program derivation/finalizer、graph publication、PassEncoder、execution capability、GraphExecutor与color contract八模块共 **82/82**；源码布局、texture candidate、frame registry、audio demand与shader census另 **28/28**，合计本批聚焦及相关回归 **110/110**。direct-red正门及`.g`、whole-vector、先alias再`.r`负门由frontend/Program锁定；publication门锁定`.scalarRedUnorm/.preservedChannels`、scalar request必须为framebuffer，以及伪造TEX `authoredFormat`、错误color purpose、rewrap到layer/effect identity拒绝；PassEncoder GPU门以四象限distinct RGBA输入证明R8只持久化red，再由direct-`.r` consumer得到预期颜色。产品[GraphExecutor GPU门](../../../script/tests/test_scene_resolved_material_graph_executor.py)从catalog claim开始，核对typed writer/reader attachment、prepare无编码副作用、完整publication、command-buffer完成、R8首末texel约64及RGBA terminal BGRA首末像素`[0,0,64,255]`；green/whole/alias、clear、unique/history、无consumer、多writer、copy、swap与repeat均以精确stage/code在frame前拒绝。channel-envelope聚合另锁定全red为true、mixed/empty为false。最终Scene全量为 **174 modules / 151.1 s / ALL OK**，code health为 **826 Swift / 44 locked legacy / 400行**；`script/build_and_run.sh verify`为`BUILD SUCCEEDED`，App使用Developer ID Application Team `H9QWU9XN8R`签名。

<a id="e-r5-b25-clean-framework-closeout"></a>
### E-R5-B25-CLEAN-FRAMEWORK-CLOSEOUT: single graph executor and stable zero-invariants

- 架构合同：产品effect路径固定为`authored graph -> SceneEffectAdmissionCatalog -> SceneEffectProgramCompiler -> frame preflight/resource publication -> SceneResolvedMaterialGraphExecutor -> SceneEffectStageRenderer -> compositor`。base layer只处理neutral source、结构性dependency与final composition；effect辅助纹理由stage descriptor精确投影，effect target只经frame preflight/persistent allocation/commit/pin lifecycle取得。`compositionTarget`只承担compositor source copy，不能成为effect logical target。
- 删除/重命名：B25删除最后3个planner observation、`SceneEffectRuntimePlan`、legacy planning decision、inline/aggregate telemetry及只服务它们的Bloom/Gradient/Perspective pipeline；旧authored chain/catalog/admission/renderer类型族改为中性的admission/program/stage execution命名并删除产品chain consumer。最终diff共224个文件、21个删除、25个完整类型族重命名，`+2052/-19585`，净减17,533行；Scene源码为 **544 Swift / 95,114 LOC / <3 KiB 180 / <1 KiB 51**。
- 稳定不变量：全部R4/R5 retirement gate均为全局`0/0`，allowed files与scope为空；另以稳定零门保护base-layer inline effect authority、heuristic shared-effect texture loading、deprecated direct target allocation与sample-specific product dispatch。合法typed leaf/compiler、main-pass structural allowlist与revision attestation单独分类，不以字面“effect/legacy”统计误判为旧执行权。
- 官方公开语义边界：现役[Effects Introduction](https://docs.wallpaperengine.io/en/scene/effects/introduction.html)确认effect按作者顺序组合并与layer连接；[Shader Syntax](https://docs.wallpaperengine.io/en/scene/shader/syntax.html)与[Shader Variables](https://docs.wallpaperengine.io/en/scene/shader/variables.html)确认自定义preprocessor、combo、明确纹理槽与host变量；[SceneScript Reference](https://docs.wallpaperengine.io/en/scene/scenescript/reference.html)确认脚本拥有独立事件/生命周期。项目据此保持ordered graph、typed slots/resources与独立script runtime边界；公开资料没有公开官方私有类层次、target allocator或像素算法，因此本锚点只声明公开语义一致，不声明内部实现复制或完全等价。
- 验证：Scene全量 **1085 tests / 586.495 s / OK / 11 skipped**；删除最终孤立helper后framebuffer **29/29 PASS**；code health **818 Swift / 44 locked legacy / 400行**；`script/build_and_run.sh verify`为`BUILD SUCCEEDED`，Developer ID deep strict codesign有效。App executable SHA-256 `c618f84e65f717485120945c3a6cfa64dbbf5acc87c14186ced661e611127a5e`、CDHash `42a4ae7f636077c5dc702df358f9d4fb28c471ec`、Team `H9QWU9XN8R`。

<a id="e-authored-overlay-alpha-blend"></a>
### E-AUTHORED-OVERLAY-ALPHA-BLEND: optional sampler presence and historical two-input Blend

- 公开/clean-room边界：官方[Shader Variables](https://docs.wallpaperengine.io/en/scene/shader/variables.html)把sampler `default`定义为作者未选其他纹理时使用的default texture，同时规定optional texture的presence combo只在真实纹理被painted/selected时为1、无绑定时为0，且读取必须位于对应`#if`内；官方[Blend](https://docs.wallpaperengine.io/en/scene/effects/effect/blend.html)只公开最多六张blend image、optional opacity mask及Write Alpha会按blend texture opacity修改底层不透明度。Mirage `da4fa7b`只作 `third-party-reference-pattern` 结构交叉验证：`ShaderAnnotations.cpp`把default texture与`texinfos.enabled`分离，`SceneCompiler.cpp`先从author material建立slot/combo，再填default。该公开字据与独立参考已经足够界定presence/default职责，本批无需Ghidra，也不复制官方/Mirage shader、payload、纹理或算法表达。
- 该提交的历史产品合同：[SceneResolvedMaterialShaderSchema.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/MaterialProgram/SceneResolvedMaterialShaderSchema.swift)只从unmarked sampler annotation保留`readinessCombo`，不会把`[COMBO]` authored marker误当sampler presence；当时[SceneResolvedMaterialTextureResolver+Launch.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/MaterialProgram/SceneResolvedMaterialTextureResolver+Launch.swift)、[SceneResolvedMaterialShaderSchema+Reachability.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/MaterialProgram/SceneResolvedMaterialShaderSchema+Reachability.swift)、[SceneResolvedMaterialRuntimeCatalog.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/SceneResolvedMaterialRuntimeCatalog.swift)与[SceneResolvedMaterialTextureResolver.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/MaterialProgram/SceneResolvedMaterialTextureResolver.swift)把combo-bearing default全面排除在reachable slot、resource demand与frame binding之外；真实author candidate存在但pending/missing/wrong-purpose时仍由原candidate失败关闭，不回退到default或combo-off。后续[E-AUTHORED-ACTIVE-DEFAULT](#e-authored-active-default)只以窄typed兼容切片取代“active combo-off default也绝不绑定”这一过强结论，default不制造presence以及candidate失败不回退继续成立。
- 颜色合同：[SceneAuthoredShaderOverlayAlphaBlendAnalyzer.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/ShaderFrontend/SceneAuthoredShaderOverlayAlphaBlendAnalyzer.swift)只分析prepared active AST，不读取effect/path/hash/sample/layer身份。它要求唯一root output、两只不同slot的唯一direct sample、已证明的normal `ApplyBlending` helper/mode 0、RGB weight精确消费overlay alpha、输出alpha只由overlay alpha与有限scalar形成，并拒绝控制流、额外sample、alias、重复/whole-vector write和base-alpha dependency；满足时复用`.straightAlpha(slot0)`。Program的既有typed门再要求slot 0为opaque/premultiplied color、其余slot为data/scalar，所以历史`rgbmask` overlay保持raw preserved RGBA，只有slot 0在作者数学前解预乘，最终输出只预乘一次。frontend schema由13升到14，旧prepared/cache identity不能越过新颜色边界。current stock `PerformBlend`的不同alpha方程仍不匹配该分析器。
- 历史自动/GPU/构建门：[test_scene_shader_color_contract.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_shader_color_contract.py)、[test_scene_resolved_material_program_finalizer.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_resolved_material_program_finalizer.py)、[test_scene_resolved_material_pass_encoder.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_resolved_material_pass_encoder.py)与[test_scene_resolved_material_execution_capability.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_resolved_material_execution_capability.py)在该提交最终联合 **34/34 PASS / 73.587 s**；当时的门矩阵把“源码无条件采样但presence off”锁为exact texture-binding拒绝，这一历史结果不能再作为现役兼容合同，现役正反门见[E-AUTHORED-ACTIVE-DEFAULT](#e-authored-active-default)。Metal 1×1 overlay-alpha oracle仍以premultiplied base `[128,0,0,128]`、preserved-data overlay `[0,255,0,64]`与两个0.5 scalar得到RGBA `[28,4,0,32]`（容差2），并在GPU前拒绝color auxiliary；该颜色门未被后续sampler修正放宽。相同产品源码的Scene全量为 **174 modules / 141.3 s / ALL OK**；最终代码健康为 **827 Swift / 44 locked legacy / 400行**，`script/build_and_run.sh verify`为`BUILD SUCCEEDED`，Developer ID deep strict codesign有效。
- 隔离运行：`/private/tmp/mwx-overlay-alpha-final.aq0M8m/report.json`使用更新后的正式full matrix只选`2938612768`，结果仍为 **0/1 NON-PASS**、process exit 0、无timeout，report/matrix SHA-256分别为`190c1d828caff385bdb1c7c4058f9ce1ea6eb999d9da7070b4652de34185806e` / `6aa3ffb541315da2faefc1deb25c180606c1dd0d5bee26a20686cc1376e5b038`。App `2.0.8 (268)` executable SHA-256 `117e32a93609b8d55417706596fe6fddc0525d750938cf794812cffb25640c95`、CDHash `ada58b55f43a50fdeab02eda06a1e684609cad1e`、Team `H9QWU9XN8R`，source及运行前后签名验证均为true。loaded ratio为1；Program layer候选由3/32提升为4/31，accepted/succeeded layers为`[280,322,390,563]`，其中layer 280的Blend 602和Opacity 1124均为`admitted-generic / complete / resolved-material / program`。首帧602以GPU completed发布中间结果且不直接上屏，1124随后GPU completed并被compositor消费；下一帧两段分别以generation 12/13再次成功，GraphExecutor failure、graph diagnostic、failed outcome与GPU failure均为0。
- 局部视觉与未闭合边界：同一旧隔离基线ready图没有满足阈值的高色度像素；本批ready/after图均出现稳定彩条，阈值像素分别为28,925/28,924，bbox同为`x=901…975, y=723…1193`，与layer 280的窄竖向作者布局一致。该same-sample变化可归因于新解锁的两stage chain，但preview comparison仍是advisory/non-gating，整景仍明显缺色。报告保留29条失败：26条既存legacy authored count/diagnostic ratchet，以及route operation与layer 299 named-target两项真实根因；dynamic uniform、forward dependency、Workshop gradient texture binding/SceneScript及其他可见effect仍未取得owner。因此本节只证明historical two-input Blend的局部可见正链，不把`2938612768`、current stock Blend、generic Blend、性能或Wallpaper Engine/Windows视觉等价升级为PASS。

<a id="e-authored-active-default"></a>
### E-AUTHORED-ACTIVE-DEFAULT: authored presence and prepared active default binding

- 公开/clean-room边界：官方[Shader Variables](https://docs.wallpaperengine.io/en/scene/shader/variables.html)分别把sampler `default`定义为作者未另选纹理时的默认资源、把optional texture combo定义为真实作者纹理的presence；公开字据要求规范作者把optional读取放入对应条件块，但没有规定combo-off prepared source若仍实际读取sampler时不得使用default。Mirage固定revision `da4fa7b`只作 `third-party-reference-pattern` 顺序交叉验证：先从material texture presence形成combo并预处理，再回填default并移除prepared inactive槽。项目不复制官方或Mirage shader、payload、纹理或算法表达；该证据已足够约束资源选择顺序，无需新增Ghidra。
- 产品合同：`readinessCombo`对应的presence/readiness bit只由真实author candidate、provider或graph source决定，combo-bearing default永不反向置该bit；non-combo default继续作为required shader-default readiness resource。frame fixed point先以author presence产生seed key，再从prepared active sampler投影资源；只有无author candidate、无graph-input alias、无formatcombo、typed asset default且purpose已证时，combo-off active sampler才可选择`.shaderDefault`。prepared inactive槽不demand/不绑定；candidate missing/pending/unavailable/wrong-purpose不回退；graph alias继续优先；formatcombo+presence-default与internal default继续失败关闭。launch envelope、resource demand、frame selection与同一variant cache共用该窄边界。
- 当前聚焦门：[test_scene_resolved_material_execution_capability.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_resolved_material_execution_capability.py)只选catalog-demand与launch-envelope两项，联合[test_scene_resolved_material_program_finalizer.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_resolved_material_program_finalizer.py)及[test_scene_graph_texture_publication.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_graph_texture_publication.py)完整class，共 **14/14 PASS / 76.189 s**。门矩阵分别锁定guarded combo-off default零demand/零binding、prepared active default typed demand与`.shaderDefault`绑定、presence mask保持仅slot 0且launch→frame仍只缓存一个variant、author off/on envelope、pending/wrong-purpose不回退、graph alias零default demand/issue、`[COMBO]` marker不冒充readiness、non-combo default保持required，以及formatcombo+presence-default零catalog demand并在preparation阶段失败关闭。该门只证明资源/variant原子，不证明Workshop gradient颜色、SceneScript、GPU输出或视觉等价。
- 当前集成/构建：统一integration门的Scene全量为 **174 modules / 157.2 s / 8 jobs / ALL OK**；code health为 **828 Swift / 44 locked legacy / 400-line limit**，`script/build_and_run.sh verify`为`BUILD SUCCEEDED`。Developer ID App executable SHA-256为`bb4de35c995a3b03a9ea8a87ef624890572cc54df603a2c32ef4f8d1a58d65b8`，CDHash为`0d56ab133245a82e6290be51311b81d6d01c64e8`，Team为`H9QWU9XN8R`。
- 隔离运行：`/private/tmp/mwx-active-default-293.jqgxlO/report.json`只选`2938612768`，结果仍为 **0/1 NON-PASS**、process exit 0、无timeout；report/full-matrix SHA-256分别为`91746be15e53b758464db090ec8b17ca3fd0fa6648a080548d7bfbbe349f4813` / `6aa3ffb541315da2faefc1deb25c180606c1dd0d5bee26a20686cc1376e5b038`。asset catalog由历史报告的`6/6 ready`变为`7/7 ready`；四个`gradient_color`拒绝由`material-variant-envelope-texture-binding count=4`精确推进为`material-variant-envelope-color-contract count=4`，不再出现该四项texture-binding首门。accepted/succeeded layers仍为`[280,322,390,563]`，GraphExecutor累计claimed/encoded/GPU encoded均为100且failure为0，12条graph observation全部terminal成功；layer 280继续首帧602中间发布、1124 terminal compositor消费并在下一帧成功。该真实报告只证明Workshop路径已越过资源首门；颜色门发生在Program发布前，所以它不证明`gradient_color`的frame binding、Program owner、GPU输出或视觉改善。报告仍保留29条失败和明显`flat-border`，不得升级样本或视觉等价。

<a id="e-media-playback-placeholder-fade"></a>
### E-MEDIA-PLAYBACK-PLACEHOLDER-FADE: bounded typed playback-state placeholder reveal

- 官方/clean-room边界：官方[media events](https://docs.wallpaperengine.io/en/scene/scenescript/reference/event/media.html)、[MediaPlaybackEvent](https://docs.wallpaperengine.io/en/scene/scenescript/reference/class/MediaPlaybackEvent.html)与[update event](https://docs.wallpaperengine.io/en/scene/scenescript/reference/event/update.html)界定state `0/1/2 = stopped/playing/paused`及逐帧动画应消费`engine.frametime`。2.8.42 固定客户端静态研究只记录被检路径的 event-before-update 候选顺序；Mirage 固定 revision 只提供 host event queue 到 script/frame 的第三方结构对照。本证据包只证明 bounded placeholder-fade owner 在项目自有顺序下执行，不把任一静态观察升级为 generic SceneScript 事件合同，也未提供 live macOS provider、颜色真值或官方等价。
- 产品合同：[SceneMediaPlaybackPlaceholderFadeProgramCompiler.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Properties/SceneMediaPlaybackPlaceholderFadeProgramCompiler.swift)只从lossless pass-owned `SceneScriptBindingIR`建立exact effect-constant target；object/effect/pass identity、完整target path、source与authored bit pattern任一漂移都不产binding，duplicate target使整program失败。[SceneMediaPlaybackPlaceholderFadeCompiler.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Properties/SceneMediaPlaybackPlaceholderFadeCompiler.swift)只接受完整`script,value` wrapper、有限scalar fallback、严格两export/变量关系/0-1-2 mapping与stopped-rise/active-fall update结构，16 KiB/1024 token门内完整消费到EOF；逆向极性、错误state、clamp、额外statement/function与超预算继续拒绝。该准入不看sample/layer/path/hash。
- 生命周期/执行：[SceneMediaPlaybackPlaceholderFadeRuntime.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Properties/SceneMediaPlaybackPlaceholderFadeRuntime.swift)以scene/host scope持每target double state；同次调用先更新event mode再用shared simulation delta推进，stopped只保留`<0 -> 0` guard、playing/paused只保留`>1 -> 1` guard，不擅自把内部counter夹到`0...1`，unknown state冻结。Host在surface循环前只推进一次并广播同一值；screen topology rebuild不重置，scene activate重建runtime。Template只把bounded compiler证明的exact target标成唯一`.sceneScript` contributor，Capability再要求同target producer/consumer，Program finalizer写入uniform bytes，普通Opacity Program、GraphExecutor与compositor未新增专用owner。
- 自动/构建：新增compiler/runtime与lossless IR projection门覆盖正向、event-before-update、非对称越界、unknown freeze、逆极性/错误mapping/clamp/预算、每一identity字段漂移与duplicate target；Template/Capability/FrameContext/ProgramFinalizer门又锁定unproven脚本仍为control、producer union/filter、host每帧一次推进与`.sceneScript` alpha bytes。checkpoint受影响 **39 modules / 62.6 s / ALL OK**；最终Scene **175 modules / 133.7 s / ALL OK**。code health **834 Swift / 44 locked legacy / 400行**，新增文件均不超过275行；`script/build_and_run.sh verify`为`BUILD SUCCEEDED`，Developer ID App executable SHA-256 `561f1a23252296dda9f13dca1255f86df83bcc1de64a9e82c820636f0a195c34`、CDHash `7c9ed48009660e3981198fffeeb9934e85095b02`、Team `H9QWU9XN8R`，source/runtime签名前后均verified。
- 两样本fresh门：更新后的full matrix SHA-256为`519ea44e4640363ae42aae8e07de9b1c74e02c4e5436e616cf79ab733833e491`。`/private/tmp/mwx-playback-fade-ratchet-293.7LZw56/report.json`为 **0/1 NON-PASS**，report SHA-256 `12814bf5a608bb99b13c17a78cdef1dfde93e8f874cb93077a6f0f92afd247a8`；loaded `44/44`、capability `7 accepted / 28 rejected`，succeeded=`[79,165,280,322,390,454,563]`，executor claimed/encoded/GPU `259/259/259`、failure 0，20条首帧/terminal/next-frame observation完成。`/private/tmp/mwx-playback-fade-ratchet-297.DdsnK0/report.json`同为 **0/1 NON-PASS**，SHA-256 `cf91d3c3709c7dd1e981115bba0ad24c98f2f0ce35f8b7dcc0f5fe5606d67641`；loaded `20/20`、capability `5/20`，succeeded=`[454,956,1481,2130,57098]`，executor `15/15/15`、failure 0，14条graph observation完成。两报告均不再有succeeded-layer mismatch；逆向`626/629/924/2094`及复杂`2011`仍不取得owner。

- 已知合成纹理债务：full45曾稳定显示`3768724269`的fade bindings为0；后续[E-GRAPH-PREFLIGHT-FAILURE-IDENTITY](#e-graph-preflight-failure-identity)隔离复跑已把旧聚合错误精确定位到`layer 163 / effect 0 / rounded_mask`的帧期`colorContractUnproven`。launch接受的17/100只因同帧原子事务未提交而一并丢失；该问题不属于本批媒体能力或收益。

<a id="e-graph-preflight-failure-identity"></a>
### E-GRAPH-PREFLIGHT-FAILURE-IDENTITY: exact late Program failure identity

- 产品诊断合同：[SceneResolvedMaterialGraphExecutor.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/EffectExecution/SceneResolvedMaterialGraphExecutor.swift)、[SceneResolvedMaterialGraphExecutor+Preparation.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/EffectExecution/SceneResolvedMaterialGraphExecutor+Preparation.swift)与[SceneResolvedMaterialGraphExecutor+Validation.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/EffectExecution/SceneResolvedMaterialGraphExecutor+Validation.swift)只扩大既有`materialFinalizerRejected`的失败身份：从已验证的current node携带`stageIndex + EffectKey + nodeIndex + materialOrdinal`，reason写入stage/layer/effect、descriptor SHA-256前12位、node/material与原始phase/code。descriptor原文不会进入日志；detail继续只取首项、最多64 UTF-8 bytes并净化为单行token。其他GraphExecutor失败、SubmissionCoordinator的`graph-preflight-`外层、execution claim、Program、Metal编码、publication与整帧no-partial-write语义均未改变。
- 自动/构建：[test_scene_resolved_material_graph_executor.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_resolved_material_graph_executor.py)以字面量digest锁定完整reason、bounded detail与既有失败枚举，定向结果 **1 module / 23.4 s / ALL OK**；code health **834 Swift / 44 locked legacy / 400行**，三个触达Swift文件均不超过397行。`script/build_and_run.sh verify`为`BUILD SUCCEEDED`，Developer ID App executable SHA-256 `48a19a901afd577e498354d86ae21eb66bc5c00db56ba78d2e8c3e91a4409b76`、CDHash `0f557b7b3d1a10e2a5573c59ab448ab418e76b61`、Team `H9QWU9XN8R`，source/runtime签名前后verified。
- 隔离定位：`/private/tmp/mwx-composition-identity-376872.9NPmRm/report.json`只选`3768724269`，结果 **0/1 NON-PASS**、exit 0、无timeout，report/full-matrix SHA-256分别为`9f193387ab2f5836c7f0822c623f90681a61849798089ec01d86aa3685e9fd77` / `519ea44e4640363ae42aae8e07de9b1c74e02c4e5436e616cf79ab733833e491`。texture为`3/3`、resolved material asset catalog为`16/16 ready`，12个active effect均complete，launch capability接受layer`[17,100,163]`；帧期唯一重复诊断精确为`graph-preflight-stage-0-layer-163-effect-0-descriptor-3cf8033eff22-node-0-material-0-finalizer-color-colorContractUnproven`。executor observation为claimed/encoded/GPU `0/0/0`、failure `1`，479条graph observation均为同一preflight诊断，窗口无non-black证据。
- 结论边界：真正违反launch→frame固定点的是layer 163的Workshop `rounded_mask`；layer 17的十stage链与layer 100的Light Shafts不是本报告识别到的Finalizer失败者，而是SubmissionCoordinator为维持整帧原子性而连带不提交。该证据闭合“资源已加载但late颜色合同使整组图像消失”的失败身份与no-partial-write归因，不证明rounded-mask语义、GPU像素、视觉修复或Wallpaper Engine等价。下一独立批应先让launch capability在签发owner前按真实typed texture content证明同一颜色合同；rounded-mask若仍需执行，再以独立AST/dataflow与GPU/样本视觉证据建立公共transfer，不能放宽现有premultiplied边界。

- 隔离执行：`/private/tmp/mwx-historical-framebuffer-155-pass.xKJb3S/report.json`只选`1553008362`，report/tracked full-matrix SHA-256分别为`6667511c7b6944831c413f2eaf258cfab58e4ea3a08c83640cce4cfac4f95c8e` / `4496d7baa7d6ce36729601e750aaf063bac864a23ebe8a0cdd878a66f905b171`，结果 **1/1 PASS**。唯一layer 12的effect 0 Water Waves先消费layerSource，effect 1/2/3 Shake逐段消费前一effectOutput；首帧和next-frame共8个graph observation均CPU encode、GPU completed且publication identity完整，前三段`compositorConsumed=false`只供下一段读取，最后一段`compositorConsumed=true`。executor累计claimed/encoded/GPU为`7/7/7`、failure/deferred均为0。

<a id="e-layer-source-launch-color-gate"></a>
### E-LAYER-SOURCE-LAUNCH-COLOR-GATE: reject one bad layerSource color stage before atomic frame submission

- 产品合同：[SceneResolvedMaterialProgram+ColorDerivation.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/MaterialProgram/SceneResolvedMaterialProgram+ColorDerivation.swift)把颜色推导收敛成同时供launch与frame使用的`ColorTextureFact`核心；[SceneMaterialAssetTextureCatalog.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Resources/SceneMaterialAssetTextureCatalog.swift)把launch前asset publication投影为保留`.ready/.absent/.pending/.unavailable`的typed状态；[SceneResolvedMaterialTextureResolver+LaunchSelection.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/MaterialProgram/SceneResolvedMaterialTextureResolver+LaunchSelection.swift)让readiness、format与color采用frame相同的候选优先级，只有explicit absent才可继续低优先级。[SceneResolvedMaterialTextureResolver+LaunchColor.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/MaterialProgram/SceneResolvedMaterialTextureResolver+LaunchColor.swift)仅对renderer-owned premultiplied `layerSource`输入建立有界content profile；该子集的稳定variant须在claim前通过同一颜色推导，否则以`material-variant-envelope-color-contract`拒绝该层。普通`effectOutput`后续stage、dynamic provider/user source与内部FBO仍保留frame精确门，不凭pixel format或purpose虚构content。
- 自动/构建：execution-capability门新增项目自有“sample RGB后独立改写alpha”形态，证明frontend可识别但premultiplied `layerSource` graph input会在launch被color-contract拒绝；asset候选门同时锁定“高优先级absent可回退、unavailable不可回退”，普通`effectOutput`输入则继续延后到frame门。原有graph/asset/default/provider与颜色正反门保持。四个直接模块 **4 modules / 88.9 s / ALL OK**，Scene全量 **175 modules / 141.4 s / ALL OK**；code health为 **836 Swift / 44 locked legacy / 400行**，两个新增Swift分别219/68行。`script/build_and_run.sh verify`为`BUILD SUCCEEDED`并使用Developer ID签名。

<a id="e-authored-straight-rgb-alpha-factor"></a>
### E-AUTHORED-STRAIGHT-RGB-ALPHA-FACTOR: bounded straight RGB with sampled-alpha coverage and layer model input

- 官方/clean-room边界：现役官方changelog只界定`g_LayerModelMatrix`是effect可读取的“unmodified model transform”；Mirage固定checkout在`SceneUniformBinder.cpp`中把effect projection source的`ModelTrans()`送入同名semantic，作为本项目从现役layer world frame取值的结构交叉验证。两者都不提供本样本shader算法或Metal payload，因此本批没有复制官方/Mirage实现，也不需要Ghidra；矩阵转置、3D坐标系与Windows逐值结果仍由后续golden界定。
- 公共产品合同：[SceneAuthoredShaderStraightRGBAlphaFactorAnalyzer.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/ShaderFrontend/SceneAuthoredShaderStraightRGBAlphaFactorAnalyzer.swift)只接受`main`内唯一直接color sample、唯一root output、无控制流/额外sample/RGB写入，并要求输出精确为`vec4(sample.rgb, sample.a * scalar factors)`的乘法链；replacement/additive alpha、条件、第二sample和颜色mutation继续返回unresolved。通过时复用既有`.straightAlpha(slot)`：Metal只在作者数学前解预乘该color slot，最终输出只预乘一次，没有新增effect/path/hash/sample dispatch。frontend schema升至15，避免旧prepared/cache identity复用新颜色结论。
- Host矩阵合同：Program host schema新增exact `float4x4 g_LayerModelMatrix`，uniform encoder继续按既有column-major矩阵编码；frame preflight从同帧`worldFramesByLayerID[layerID]`取得当前layer world transform并贯穿RuntimeBridge、GraphExecutor与Program finalizer，缺失时整层失败关闭，不用identity、geometry size、MVP或effect texture projection猜值。该值包含现役2D parent/local动态transform合同，但不据此声明任意3D/effect/view矩阵兼容。
- 自动/GPU/构建：颜色门覆盖sampled-alpha乘法正例及replacement、additive、conditional、extra-sample、RGB-mutation负例；终审后又加入reachable-helper闭包证明，helper内隐藏sample/control-flow/`out`/`inout`/外部写继续拒绝，纯标量helper仍可用。真实Metal 1x1门以premultiplied `[64,32,16,128]`和coverage `0.5`得到`[32,16,8,64]`，同时锁定prepare/encode/GPU完成。Program finalizer以非identity对角矩阵锁定`g_LayerModelMatrix`字节，frame接线门锁定world-frame来源。Scene全量 **175 modules / 179.5 s / ALL OK**；helper收紧后相关6模块再次 **ALL OK**，code health **837 Swift / 44 locked legacy / 400行**，新增analyzer 305行；`script/build_and_run.sh verify`再次为`BUILD SUCCEEDED`。当前Developer ID App executable SHA-256为`f3582a7929fc131a28a359ea44e040b074d80f4b6639a47a632bb752081b012a`、CDHash为`31a4f6199e4b13303768403259f4ee87c105f709`、Team为`H9QWU9XN8R`，source/runtime签名前后均verified。

- 公共颜色合同：提交`9977d5855461ef903f3987f075f76b91d6a3bd11`在[SceneAuthoredShaderStraightRGBScalarAlphaAnalyzer.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/ShaderFrontend/SceneAuthoredShaderStraightRGBScalarAlphaAnalyzer.swift)与[SceneAuthoredShaderStraightRGBScalarAlphaAnalyzer+Scalar.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/ShaderFrontend/SceneAuthoredShaderStraightRGBScalarAlphaAnalyzer+Scalar.swift)中加入不读取effect/path/hash/sample/layer identity的结构证明。它只接受唯一直接sampled color及其alias、RGB除既有非负clamp外保持原样、唯一一次`color.a *= scalar`，以及由`1...3`个互异且非color slot的direct `.r` data/scalar auxiliary sample组成的有限scalar graph；`.g`/whole-vector、重复slot、RGB mutation、alpha replacement、额外color sample、未知helper与控制流继续unresolved。通过时复用`.straightAlpha(slot)`：作者数学前只解预乘color slot，辅助data不解预乘，末端只预乘一次；Program仍拒绝color auxiliary。frontend schema从16升到17，旧prepared/cache identity不能越过新结论。

<a id="e-layer-source-passthrough"></a>
### E-LAYER-SOURCE-PASSTHROUGH: fail-closed source-stage visibility for unclaimed effects

- 公共合同：[SceneLayerSourcePassthroughPlan.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Rendering/SceneLayerSourcePassthroughPlan.swift)只对exact `.unclaimed`路由建计划，并要求可见image层、不存在已授权/可执行typed dependency、source-copy、frame target或final-alpha，neutral alpha/tint/brightness/color/blend/texture-frame，exact `.layerSource(layerID)` static-file或current-media publication atom，以及有限且与viewport有正面积交集的authored quad。[SceneImageLayerDrawRequest.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Rendering/SceneImageLayerDrawRequest.swift)把coverage/opacity资源继续默认为source coverage blocker；已证例外只有：exact `effects/pulse/effect.json`单一pass 0、slots `[nil, util/noise, optional local mask]`与paths一致、无user texture、bounded stock combo且`PULSEALPHA`缺省/`0`；以及由definition/material/shader strict identity准入的stock `effects/waterwaves/effect.json`单一pass 0、slots `[nil, optional local mask]`与paths一致、无user texture、`TIMEOFFSET/PERSPECTIVE/DUALWAVES`缺省/`0`。Pulse alpha分支关闭时局部mask不改变source alpha；Water Waves slot-1 mask只调制被跳过effect的采样位移，是effect-local displacement input而非独立opacity/final-alpha law，本降级不声称effect输出与raw source逐像素coverage恒等。`PULSEALPHA=1`即使mask未加载、非法pass/path/combo、未知资源形态，任何typed Opacity entry即使纹理缺失，以及非stock Water Waves identity、非零combo、多pass/错pass下的loaded mask都继续阻断。[SceneDependencyRenderPlan.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/LayerDependencies/SceneDependencyRenderPlan.swift)与[SceneDependencyFrameRuntime.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/LayerDependencies/SceneDependencyFrameRuntime.swift)从可见consumer的显式dependency与named reference建立安全集合，static-file provider和consumer两端只要存在authored edge就都不得passthrough；hidden consumer、self reference和unreferenced layer不阻断。current-media保留先前独立审查的降级authority，不受这个static-only角色门影响。[SceneImageLayerCompositor.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Rendering/SceneImageLayerCompositor.swift)仅把准入source以作者MVP交给普通final layer compose，记录`degraded-layer-source-passthrough`；不执行任何effect、不发布`effectOutput`、不恢复partial prefix、不消费authored dependency，也不把该路径记为effect成功。
- X-Ray资源身份与实例门：[SceneAuthoredXRayPlanner+StockIdentity.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/SceneAuthoredXRayPlanner+StockIdentity.swift)登记current stock与exact historical stock两条公共资源身份。每条身份都在同一profile内原子核对完整typed definition variant、唯一typed material pass、root-stage canonical identity、VFS sourceGraph的stored/recomputed dependency、全部node source自哈希/byte count，以及唯一stock `shaders/common_blending.h` include；current/historical不能混配。digest只用于验证登记资源内容完整性，能力由共享X-Ray语义和后续typed实例形状取得，不按sample/layer/hash分派算法。current身份继续服务既有X-Ray execution planner与consumer降级；historical身份只允许consumer显示自己的base source，不取得execution owner。[SceneDependencyGraphAnalysis.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/RenderGraph/LayerDependencies/SceneDependencyGraphAnalysis.swift)再要求可见image层全部跨层输入精确等于一个真实存在、非self provider的单一pass-0 slot-1 primary blend texture（`_rt_imageLayerComposite_<P>_a`，`P`==唯一authored dependency），slot 0为nil、slot 2非named reference、`BLENDMODE/OPACITYMASK`缺省/`0`、无user texture、无第二个named reference。两版已登记`xray.frag`都保持consumer自身alpha，只把provider RGB混入pointer halo，因此provider输入属于被跳过effect的effect-local输入；provider端仍一律阻断（`1636394814:24`保持failed），exempt consumer若同时是其他可见consumer的provider也仍阻断。
- 自动/GPU门：[test_scene_framebuffer_capture.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_framebuffer_capture.py)的真实Metal门保留Pulse缺省/显式`PULSEALPHA=0`、loaded local mask、stock Water Waves loaded mask与static-file/current-media first/next-frame精确像素正门，并锁定各自非法combo/pass/path/identity、typed Opacity及static cross-layer角色负门；原有作者局部quad、rotation、partial clipping、identity/generation/style/geometry与其他mask负门不变。[test_scene_xray_planner.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_xray_planner.py)直接编译production stock-identity matcher，锁定current/historical exact profile、effectIndex 10、typed definition/material、root canonical重算、sourceGraph dependency重算、stock include provenance/source、node byte count与hybrid负门；current execution helper还单独拒绝mutated dependency/include及historical contract。[test_scene_dependency_render_plan.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_dependency_render_plan.py)锁定visible named/explicit edge的provider+consumer双端阻断，以及provider存在的verified X-Ray consumer正门与缺失authority/provider、额外dependency、非零combo、错slot/reference/pass、consumer再作为provider等负门。最终源码先通过`test_scene_xray_planner`、`test_scene_dependency_render_plan`、`test_scene_semantics_coverage`、`test_scene_runtime_input`四个exact modules **ALL OK**，再通过统一checkpoint focused tests **34 modules / 83.6 s / ALL OK**、Developer ID `script/build_and_run.sh verify` **BUILD SUCCEEDED**、Scene全量 **179 modules / 160.0 s / ALL OK**；code health为 **866 Swift files / 11 locked legacy / 44 pre-existing warnings / PASS**。下方fresh report生成于这些最终源码与build之后。该结构/真实route证据仍没有per-route publication/compositor/next-frame观察，不能表述为X-Ray plan→GPU first/next-frame闭环。[scene_wallpaper_benchmark.py](../../../script/scene_wallpaper_benchmark.py)继续把已编码passthrough记为degraded failure，因此样本不会因看见base source而误报PASS。
- 297隔离运行：`/private/tmp/mwx-layer-source-sentinels.sYq914/out-2974757317-guarded/report.json`的SHA-256为`89f64f8991171e35e6b01b422ac154cbd54357e16dc82654cbe543ff38d526f1`，隔离matrix SHA-256为`518fdaa2982b8e7e13396e8e4c0d628c8f177f04f6a788b4f2644a5d5e17404b`。Developer ID App executable SHA-256为`1c3f56ba6949ab9c04830c80cc3a66102db283425fe2e5fe6b019b53d4a37d23`、CDHash为`dd3dcc3e35839baa0a2524946521817499b02cef`、Team为`H9QWU9XN8R`，source/runtime签名前后均verified。报告明确为 **0/1 NON-PASS**：failures包含degraded passthrough、route operation failed与既存text binding count/ID mismatch；layers `1200/1314`记录degraded encoded，layer `1018`仍以`unclaimed-visible-effects` failed。`1200/1314`在raw文档中仍有dependency声明，但本降级没有可执行typed dependency owner、也没有消费这些边；后续named/media chain仍未执行。
- 可见ROI：运行只把项目自有`512×512 RGBA`四象限PNG作为current-media fixture（SHA-256 `481c7b10907eb34e5c8c798a60e3a4abaa24d79706cad6f5188b7cefba113027`），官方客户端截图没有作为产品或测试输入。3024×1964 ready/after中，64 px patch median在`(1307,720)/(1717,720)`分别为红/绿，`(1307,1131)/(1717,1131)`分别为蓝/黄，而同y的外层guard `x=1040/1980`仍为红/绿；这只证明中央source按authored cover quad覆盖了外层背景，不是旧的full-viewport replacement。after截图SHA-256为`2f660f38d9a9a1f53d22a4900089a2db9c9a57dd3c41607859fc1432696384c1`。
- Pulse/static隔离运行：`/private/tmp/mwx-pulse-passthrough.86eYBD/out/report.json`的SHA-256为`e46f5ef7f15d6085494848435ebff5906d8a74756c2547346d0821461262e230`，full matrix SHA-256为`d11f1aa079f3245661229c540b7c598fb3f5476a0229ff14591b4cbba5cbce91`。Developer ID App executable SHA-256为`34e6e54cf63332258e0ace9f48f267187574e50e3d11d36ae933fd3b3cada681`、debug dylib SHA-256为`835321bb0694252c1ded1798eb3a9727c8623de23225452d04e1a6fa717e6a2d`、CDHash为`35555bd2ef29bab7344001853b3a9164fd49fa01`、Team为`H9QWU9XN8R`，source/runtime签名前后均verified。两个定向sample均刻意为 **0/2 NON-PASS**。`2241938645:68`含3个exact stock Pulse、combos `{}`、slots `[nil, util/noise, mask]`且`PULSEALPHA`缺省为0；它记录`degraded-layer-source-passthrough / encoded`，shared frame 0 completed，after截图SHA-256为`a7692ba48b4dc6a6a8e41aa1f978d1e81fd916da220afcf76384438d89acdded`。与同为3024×1964的官方客户端截图（SHA-256 `57a2746294d83fca54d4d06538459249afc614660464d23ad05bd9949437e6b0`）直接对照只支持“基础摩托车构图方向一致”；report中Steam preview方向性`spatial color/luminance/histogram similarity = 0.9859/0.9883/0.9336`。10个unsupported effects未执行，所以这不是Pulse或整景视觉等价。`1636394814`当时记录1条utility dependency edge；layers `24/195`均为`unclaimed-effect-product-authority / failed / unclaimed-visible-effects`，没有passthrough，shared frame 0仍completed。其after截图SHA-256为`35dc051e942189c389353e1a67707aebf68e5435f8e7a97a847224cead35e4a0`，黑洞与NON-PASS仍在；该批只证明没有把authored X-Ray provider/consumer误放成原始静态图，不证明该样本恢复。随后一次签名App下`1636394814`的新结果见下方X-Ray authority最终隔离运行；它不自动代表后续HEAD。
- X-Ray authority最终隔离运行：fresh report为`/private/tmp/mwx-xray-authority-material-final.O1GZoJ/report.json`，SHA-256 `41117c8053c3ba3810f31ebf530792dd6c7facda08718ebb380713e2ebaeb4f9`，full matrix SHA-256 `d11f1aa079f3245661229c540b7c598fb3f5476a0229ff14591b4cbba5cbce91`。它运行的是最终源码之后的Developer ID App：executable SHA-256 `f0550677b0326c5443fc29a160c794076bc0f5096735c5b8f7cc939c3061de84`、CDHash `d9fca931d6ef55c81a81c7236a31be41c2f7e29a`、Team `H9QWU9XN8R`，`source_signature_verified/verified_before/verified_after`均为true。结果明确为 **0/2 NON-PASS**。`1636394814` frame 0记录layer `195` `degraded-layer-source-passthrough / encoded`、layer `24` `unclaimed-effect-product-authority / failed / unclaimed-visible-effects`，shared frame 0 `completed`；同一App日志仍记录`MWX authored X-Ray rejected layer=195 reason=shape-or-declaration`，证明exact historical stock身份没有被提升为current X-Ray execution owner。`2241938645` frame 0记录layer `68` `degraded-layer-source-passthrough / encoded`，shared frame 0同样`completed`。两个样本的effect execution均为attempted `0`、returned outputs `0`。
- 可见对照：四张窗口图均为3024×1964。`2241938645`最终after SHA-256为`289671595d6478856b47987124f71e4da7a8b70f2c7c9dd2eff745eada392628`；与官方客户端截图SHA-256 `57a2746294d83fca54d4d06538459249afc614660464d23ad05bd9949437e6b0`目视对照，基础摩托车构图、裁切和主色已高度接近，但未执行的authored effects仍不允许升级为整景等价。`1636394814`最终after SHA-256为`5b129dce688569ae9fae077585f1ecdeb28e54bb61fa343bf66b957846858ff2`；与官方客户端截图SHA-256 `38c746b3140b47fcb01edaf1b790064d6e30fc7e5f9873cae0cc87430238fe1f`目视对照，consumer基础人物图已从黑洞恢复，但上下裁切不同，X-Ray及其他动态/合成效果没有恢复，不能称效果或整样本等价。benchmark内置Steam preview比较仅为directional/advisory、非gating：`1636394814` spatial color/luminance similarity为`0.7150/0.7122`，`2241938645`为`0.9856/0.9882`；这些数值不能替代同帧官方golden、per-effect或per-route证据。
- 严格边界：项目自有GPU harness可精确证明static/current-media publication atom、compositor encode以及first/next-frame输出；但fresh `1636394814/2241938645`真实日志只有各自route operation与shared frame 0 command-buffer completion，后者不能归因到单个passthrough，也没有该路径专属的exact publication identity、terminal compositor consumption或next-frame观察。因此fresh report只验证最终App下的fail-closed路由、基础图可见性与有界目视方向，不证明historical X-Ray execution、Pulse/Water Waves/X-Ray效果支持、完整样本PASS、官方像素/动态等价或Wallpaper Engine parity。`1636394814:24`继续是cross-layer provider负向sentinel；`3290491250`是既有resolved Sharpen GPU/publication/compositor/next-frame正证，不是本降级批次的sentinel。

<a id="e-video"></a>
### E-VIDEO: 内嵌 MP4 image-layer 子集

- 实现提交：lifecycle `34212326`，普通文件 SourceKey/revision 门 `7866dbc0`。代码：[SceneVideoProviderLifecycleState.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Resources/SceneVideoProviderLifecycleState.swift)、[SceneVideoTextureSource.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Resources/SceneVideoTextureSource.swift)、[SceneVideoTextureSourceRegistry.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Resources/SceneVideoTextureSourceRegistry.swift)、[SceneFrameLayerTextureAssembly.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Rendering/SceneFrameLayerTextureAssembly.swift)、[SceneDesktopWallpaperHost+VideoProviders.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Runtime/SceneDesktopWallpaperHost+VideoProviders.swift) 与 [SceneTextureLoader.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Resources/SceneTextureLoader.swift)。registry 现在以同一个 canonical path/size/mtime/device/inode/ctime SourceKey 建 identity，并在 cache hit、新建 source 前后复核；metadata 不可得或 revision 中途变化时不登记、不复用、不发布 source。
- 自动门：[test_scene_video_texture_source.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_video_texture_source.py)、[test_scene_frame_context.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_frame_context.py)、[test_scene_frame_texture_registry.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_frame_texture_registry.py) 与 [test_scene_wallpaper_benchmark.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_wallpaper_benchmark.py)。最终 video source 门 **7/7**；纯 lifecycle 门锁定 SceneClock -> item-time 确定映射、同 frame decode/publication 去重、成功 publication 才增 generation、pause 保帧、resume/rebuild 连续、launch epoch 与 stop 幂等；source/ownership 门锁定 metadata unavailable fail-closed、完整 file revision 的 cache/new-source 双复核、prepare-without-autoplay、首帧启动失败重锚、launch-scoped per-layer/source/device identity、surface rebuild 复用、过期 source/observer/player/cache/临时文件 teardown。
- 运行门：`34212326` 的签名 Debug App 运行 `.codex/scene-video-provider-lifecycle-targeted-20260730-v7/report.json`，`3743305891` **1/1 PASS**，报告 SHA-256 `2167dbd6af183414458bd393efa8137b1e4aaec356e8f516fa0c5e596b7fe57c`。preview 日志记录 layer `147/23` 两路 MP4 source ready；ready/6 秒 after 均非黑，`mean_delta=0.075302`、`changed_ratio=0.821885`，completed frame `209`、failed frame `0`、driver `59.82 FPS`，stop surface `1 -> 0`。方向性 montage 的 spatial color/luminance similarity 均约 `0.942`，人工未见黑块、异常裁切或 alpha 破坏；它不证明逐帧 Windows 对齐。
- 边界：真实门证明启动、连续运动、effect 组合与 stop，但来自 `34212326`；`7866dbc0` 没有新增真实 AVPlayer 或样本门。pause/resume、screen hot-plug/surface rebuild identity、EOF loop 首帧和 codec/device-loss 尚未做真实 AVPlayer 运行注入，只由纯状态/接线门约束。seek 未实现；首个全黑 decoded frame 尚不延迟呈现；同 device/source 可跨 surface 复用，不同 Metal device 仍各自持有 source。没有跨网络/粗粒度 metadata 的 content digest、A/V sync、色彩空间、HDR 或 Windows golden，不能外推为 generic video/material provider 或 Texture Variant。

<a id="e-lifecycle"></a>
### E-LIFECYCLE: switch / stop / diagnostics

- 代码：[SceneDesktopWallpaperHost.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Runtime/SceneDesktopWallpaperHost.swift)、[SceneDiagnostics.swift](../../../MyWallpaperX/Core/SteamWorkshopScene/Runtime/SceneDiagnostics.swift)
- 自动门：[test_scene_wallpaper_benchmark.py](../../../script/tests/test_scene_wallpaper_benchmark.py)
- 运行门：所有正式矩阵样本退出后 `surface=0`，并记录 timeout、resource、graph、GPU 和 unsupported diagnostics；尚无 30 分钟交互或 2 小时 soak。

## 3. 使用规则

1. 专项表的 `L3` 行必须直接链接本页某个证据包，或自行给出代码、自动测试和运行证据三联。若证据包或提交声称修复用户可见显示，同一隔离报告还必须证明目标 layer/stage 的 GPU completed、精确 publication identity、terminal compositor consumed、next-frame 再成功和预先指定 ROI，并列出仍被拒绝或未实现的层。
2. 一个证据包只能证明标题和边界中写明的子集；不能横向给同名但未消费的官方能力升级。报告的 non-black、exit 0、loaded/route/claim count、矩阵计数和进程存活只能作诊断，不能代替目标 ROI 或官方视觉 golden。
3. 先在稳定源码上完成一次终审，再生成新的隔离报告，最后同步本页、受影响专题表和总台账；只有该批另有明确登记的现役计划时才同步计划。不得用移动快照上的连续审计或旧 App/旧报告替代最终运行证据。
4. 报告丢失、样本集变化或 App 身份变化时，保留历史数字但不得写成当前已复核事实。
